./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:15:A seed is reusable source/pathing structure that can be composed with the user's local life context, source authority, path state, deadline physics, reflow constraints, and Step Quality Firewall. ./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:49:PLOS seed taxonomy includes:
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:53:| starter seeds | Low-risk first action when full path authority is incomplete. | Must be labeled as starter guidance, not full source-backed pathing. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:54:| capability seeds | Reusable capability building blocks. | Must retain source, claim, requirement, and freshness links. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:55:| proof seeds | Evidence or receipt requirements needed before, during, or after execution. | Must bind to proof requirements and privacy class. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:56:| requirement seeds | Required conditions, documents, equipment, rules, or constraints. | Must carry requirement source state and review/freshness state. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:57:| prerequisite seeds | Before-you-start dependencies. | Must block or sequence Steps when missing. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:58:| recovery seeds | Safe recovery routes after interruption, failure, or changed reality. | Must avoid shame and preserve proof/reflow receipts. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:59:| replacement seeds | Alternative path candidates when a Step or path no longer fits. | Must preserve source authority and reason for replacement. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:60:| elasticity seeds | Shrink, extend, defer, split, merge, or re-scope behavior. | Must respect source, risk, and deadline envelopes. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:61:| path overlay seeds | Reusable overlays for role, path, requirement, proof, or projection variants. | Must identify dependency packs and overlay IDs. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:62:| momentum-tail seeds | Small follow-through actions that keep continuity after completion. | Must be optional and not become streak/score pressure. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:63:| jurisdiction seeds | Jurisdiction, age, eligibility, school, legal, travel, certification, or rule-specific constraints. | Must block authoritative pathing when jurisdiction is unknown. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:64:| deadline-protection seeds | Actions or reflows that protect a date or deadline. | Must be tied to deadline physics and source authority. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:65:| resource-light seeds | Lower-capacity alternatives when time, energy, money, access, or attention is constrained. | Must not erase proof or safety requirements. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:66:| location-compatible seeds | Location, access, mobility, or environment-compatible variants. | Must preserve privacy and local context boundaries. |
./docs/codex/SEED_BASED_PLANNING_LAW.md:67:| split/merge seeds | Decompose or combine Steps when scale changes. | Must keep receipts and source traceability. |

Public-reference packs and reusable seeds must pass source binding, duplicate/contradiction, freshness, revocation, risk/jurisdiction, private-data leak, seed-coverage, Step Quality preflight, rollback, and release-receipt gates before future staging or release; live R2 promotion remains forbidden unless a future active AMB issue explicitly owns that scope.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:12:- 2026-06-12: AMB-668 / PLOS-040 defines the R2 layout as immutable public Source Atlas distribution, not personal storage. Production object keys must be content-addressed and ring-scoped; current pointers live in signed manifests/indexes; allowed families are packs, seeds, manifests, freshness, revocations, compatibility, validation reports, release receipts, and rollback manifests. User goals, captures, schedules, proof, receipts, replay, local learning, diagnostics, exports, private imports, identifiers, raw private text, secrets, and write tokens are structurally forbidden from keys, bodies, metadata, requests, logs, and support artifacts.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:18:- 2026-06-12: AMB-663 / PLOS-032 defines signing trust states as active, rotating, deprecated, revoked, or unknown. Unknown or compromised trust fails closed; revoked keys block production eligibility; rollback and last-known-good fallback require exact artifact ids, hashes, signer ids, release receipts, and non-revoked verification. This is policy/control-plane foundation only, not key infrastructure, signer trust model implementation, R2 publication, release recovery proof, or security certification.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:24:- 2026-06-12: AMB-658 / PLOS-025 defines R2 as source-only public-reference/source/pathing distribution. R2 may store public Source Atlas pack manifests, source records, claims/requirements, reusable seeds, freshness manifests, revocation/quarantine metadata, release receipts, and compatibility metadata only when non-user-specific and source-bound. R2 must not store or receive user goals, captures, schedules, proof, receipts, replay, local learning, user mini-packs, exports, diagnostics, identifiers, raw private text, inferred priorities, or private source-needed context.
./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:46:- 2026-06-12: AMB-640 installs the Step Elasticity Runtime Law as supporting PLOS authority. ./artifacts/plos-runtime/PLOS_DECISIONS.md:47:- 2026-06-12: AMB-639 installs Source Atlas Authority and Seed-Based Planning laws as supporting PLOS authority.
